1b1(1): @#//bam po sum cu gnyis pa////'thab pa bzhes bya ba ni/gleng gzhi mnyan du yod pa na bzhugs te/de'i tshe yul ko sa la dang /yul ma ga d+ha'i
1b2(1): bar mtshams na/seng ge mthu rtsal bdo ba gnyis 'dug ste/de gnyis de na 'dug pas/skye bo mang po kha bdog par byed de/'phrul bcad do//de gnyis kyis la ma chod pas/su yang lam der 'gro mi nus
1b3(1): par gyur to//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bsgrod pa gcig pa'i lam ston pa rnam pa gnyis kyi shes bya dang ye shes la mnga' brnyes pa/ma 'dres pa'i dran pa nya bar gzhag pa gsum la mkhas pa/mi 'jigs
1b4(1): pa bzhis mi 'jigs pa/'gro ba lngar grol ba las rnam par grol ba/skye mched drug la mkhas pa/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun la spyod yul ba/rnam par thar pa brgyad la bsam gtan pa/mthar
1b5(1): gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la snyoms par 'jug pa/stobs bcu'i stobs dang ldan pa/yang dag pa'i seng ge'i sgra chen po sgrogs pa rnams ni chos nyid kyis nyin dus gsum ste/
1b6(1): nyin mtshan dus drug tu sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis 'jig rten la gzigs te/su ni rgud/su ni dar/su ni 'phongs shing 'dug/su ni nyam nga bar gyur/su ni gnod pa dang ldan/su ni phongs pa dang /
1b7(1): nyam nga ba dang /gnod pa dang ldan/su ni ngan song du gzhol/su ni ngan song du 'bab/su ni ngan song du bab/su ni ngas ngan song nas phyung ste/mtho ris dang thar pa dang 'bras bu la gzhag par bya/su ni
1b8(1): nyes par spyod pa'i 'dam du bying ba las lag nas dang bar bya/su ni 'phags pa'i nor bdun med pa las 'phags pa'i nor bdun gyi dbang phyug la dbang byed du gzhug par bya/su ni dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams ma
1b9(1): bskyed pa las bskyed par bya/su ni dge ba'i rtsi ba rnams bskyed zin pa las smin par bya/su ni dge ba'i rtse ba rnams smin zin pa las ye shes kyi mtshon gyis brtol bar bya/su la ni sangs rgyas byung 
2a1(1): @#//bas brgyan pa'i 'jig rten 'bras bu yod par bya snyam du ye shes gzigs pa 'jug go/rgya mtsho chu srin rnams kyi gnas//dus rlabs yol bar 'gyur yang srid//gdul
2a2(1): bar bya ba'i sras rnams la//sangs rgyas dus las yol ba med//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis seng ge de gnyis gdul ba'i dus la bab par dgongs nas/tshe dang ldan pa kun dga' bo la bka' stsal pa/kun dga' bo
2a3(1): dge slong rnams la bcom ldan 'das yul ma ga d+ha'i phyogs su ljongs rgyu zhing gshegs kyis/su bcom ldan 'das dang lhan cig ljongs rgyu zhing 'gro 'dod pa de dag chos gos dang chos shig ces sgo shig cos bka' stsal
2a4(1): nas|bcom ldan 'das dbyer gnas shing /dbyer bzhugs nas/dge slong mang pos zham ring byas te/dge slong gi dge 'dun gyis mdun du bdar nas rgyal po'i khab tu chas te mthar gyis rgyu zhing 
2a5(1): gshegs gshegs nas/seng ge'i nags khung du byin nas/seng ge'i de gnyis zas tshol du song song ba la de gnyis phrad nas 'thab par chas so//bcom ldan 'das kyang de gnyis la thugs brtse ba'i phyir mdun
2a6(1): logs su gzhes so//de nas thag ring po zhig nas seng ge'i de gnyis kyis bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang seng+ge de gnyis kyi khro ba'i kun nas dkris pa med par gyur te/bcom ldan 'das la
2a7(1): shin tu dga' bar gyur na/de gnyis bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la go bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal du lta zhing 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan
2a8(1): 'das kyis de gnyis la bka' stsal pa/bzhin bzangs khyed gnyis sngon gyi nyes par spyod pa'i las kyis dud 'gro'i nang du skyes na/khyed gnyis da ltar yang de 'ba' shig gi rgyu byed cing 'dug go/bzhin bzangs
2a9(1): gnyis 'di ltar 'du byed thams cad ni mi rtag go/chos thams cad ni bdag med do//mya ngan las 'das pa ni zhi ba'o//nga la sems dga' bar gyis shig dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas dang bral bar 'gyur yang srid do/
2b1(1): zhes bka' stsal nas/bcom ldan 'das gshegs so//de gnyis bcom ldan 'das las chos thos nas kha 'tshos so//dud 'gro'i skye gnas su skyes pa rnams ni me'i drod che ste/bcom ldan 'das la sems dga'
2b2(1): ba skyes bzhin du shi nas/lha'i nang du skyes so//lha'i bu 'am lha'i bu mo skyes ma thag pa ni chos nyid kyis sems rnam pa gsum skye bar 'gyur te/bdag gang nas shi 'phos gang du skyes/las gang gis skye snyam
2b3(1): mo||de nas dud 'gro las shis 'phos pa dang /lha'i nad du skyes pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la sem dga' bar gyur bar de nyid kyis mthong ngo ///de nas des gnyis kyis bsams pa/bdag cag gnyis
2b4(1): zhag lon nas/bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i 'gro ba ni/bdag cag gnyis kyi cha ma yin gyis bdag cag gnyis zhag mdun lon par bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i phyir 'gro'o snyam mo//de nas sngon seng ger gyur
2b5(1): pa ni lha'i bu rna cha gdub kor dag cing 'gul pa thogs pa/lus do shal dang /se mo'i do'i rgyan pa/nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyis spras pa'i cod pan can/lus gur kum dang ta ma la'i lo ma dang /sbrig ka
2b6(1): las sogs pas byugs pa/de gnyis kyi de nyid mtshan mo lha'i me tog ud pa la dang /pad ma dang /ku mu da dang /pad ma dkar po dang /man dar ba rnams kyis thu pa bkang ste/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal 
2b7(1): thams cad 'od chen pos snang bar byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la me tog gis gtor te/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas/chos mnyan pa'i phyir spyan sngar 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan
2b8(1): 'das kyis de gnyis la de dang 'thun pa'i chos bstan te de gnyis kyis chos thos nas bden pa mthong bar gyur te/de gnyis bden pa mthong nas rang gi gnas su song ngo //de nas dge slong rnams kyis rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi
2b9(1): tshal thams cad snang ba chen pos khyab par gyur pa mthong nas bcom ldan 'das la zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das ci lags/mdangs gsum mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa 'am/lha'i dbang po brgya byin nam/'jig
3a1(1): @#//rten skyong ba bzhi 'am/lha'i bu rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba gzhan 'ga' zhig bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i slad du/mchis lags sam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag
3a2(1): mdangs gsum mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa 'am/lha'i dbang po brgya byin nam/'jig rten skyong ba bzhi 'am/lha bu rdzu 'phrul che zhing ma thu che ba gzhan 'ga' zhigs blta ba'i phyir 'ongs pa ma yin gyi/
3a3(1): dge slong khyed kyis nags khung dgon pa na/seng+ge gnyis phan tshun 'thab cing 'dug pa mthong ngo //gsol pa/btsun pa mthong lags so//bka' stsal pa/ngas de gnyis la chos bstan te/de gnyis kyis
3a4(1): nga las chos thos nas kha 'tshos te/de gnyis shi 'phos te dus las 'das pa las lha'i nang du skyes te/lhar gyur nas nga'i thad du 'ongs te/de gnyis la ngas chos bstan nas/nga la chos thos pa des na de

3a6(1): ris dang thar pa thob par mdzad la/yul 'khor gnyis kyi 'jigs pa bsal ba la gzigs/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/da ltar 'ba' shig ma yin te/'das pa'i dus na yang ngas 'di gnyis kyi khon
3a7(1): byang bar byas nas/bsam gtan bzhi dang /mngon par shes pa lnga la bkod de/yul 'khor gnyis kyis 'jigs pa yang ji ltar bsal ba de nyon cig/dge slong dag sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na/yul ka
3a8(1): shi'i rgyal po tshangs pas byin dang /bi de ha'i rgyal po dbang chen sde gnyis phan tshun mi 'thun par gyur nas/de gnyis kyis dus su skye bo phal po che kha 'dog par byed do//de nas phyi zhig na de gnyis kyis dpung gi tshogs
3a9(1): yan lag bzhi go bskon nas/yul dang 'gyod par 'dod pa'i phyir so mtshams na 'khod do//de gnyis 'khod pa'i 'dab na dka' thub kyi nags shig na/drang srong mngon par shes pa lnga ldan pa/rdzu 'phrul che zhing 
3b1(1): mthu che zhig gnas pa des/de gnyis la snying rtse ba'i phyir/steng gi nam mkha' las 'ongs nas/de gnyis kyi bar mtshams su 'dug go/de nas drang srong de mthong nas/de gnyis kyi khro ba'i kun nas dkris pa med par gyur 
3b2(1): nas;drang srong de la shas cher dga' bar gyur to//de gnyis dga' bar gyur nas/drang srong de la smras pa/bka' stsal pa ci mchis/drang srong des smras pa/khon byang bar gyis shig/rgyal po de gnyis smras pa/drang 
3b3(1): srong gis ji skad bka' stsal pa de bzhin du tshal lo zhes smras nas/'jal dum byas te/drang srong gi rkang pa la phyag 'tshal nas/chos mnyan pa'i phyir/drang srong gi mdun du 'dug go/drang srong des kyang de gnyis la
3b4(1): de dang 'thun po'i chos bstan te/des na rgyal po de gnyis so so'i bu rab rgyal sar bcug nas/drang srong de nyid kyi thad du rab tu byung ste/bsam gtan bzhi dang /mngon par shes pa lnga skyed do//dge slong dag ji 
3b5(1): snyam du sems/de'i tshe drang srong du gyur pa byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la gnas pa gang yin pa de ni nga nyid yin no//de'i tshe ka shi'i rgyal po tshangs pas byin dang /bi de ha'i rgyal po dbang chen sngar gyur pa gang yin pa de 
3b6(1): gnyis ni seng+ge 'di gnyis kho na yin te/de'i tshe yang ngas de gnyis kyi khon byang bar byas nas/rab tu byung ste/bsam gta bzhi dang /mngon par zhes pa lnga bskyed do//da ltar yang ngas 'dis gnyis kyi khon byang ba byas 
3b7(1): nas;mtho ris thar pa thob par byas so//gsol pa/btsun pa 'di gnyis kyis las ci bgyis na/las de'i rnam par smin pas seng ger skyes lags/las ci bgyis na de nas shi 'phos nas lha'i nang du skyes la/'di gnyis
3b8(1): kyis bcom ldan 'das mnyes par bgyis te/mi gnyes par ma bgyis lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi gsung rab la 'di gnyis dge bsnyen du gyur te/kha mi
3b9(1): mkhas la der 'di gnyis khros pas skye bo mang po la dud 'gro zhes bya ba'i tshig smras la/der 'di gnyis kyis tshe gcig tu skyabs su 'gro ba dang /bslab pa'i gzhi rnams bsrungs nas/'chi kar smon lam btab pa yang 
4a1(1): @#//dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gis bram ze'i khye'u bla ma lung bstan pa gang yin pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de bdag gis mnyes par byed par gyur cig mi nyams 
4a2(1): mi mnyes par byed par ma gyur cig ces byas so//dge slong dag ji snyam du sems/de'i tshe dge snyen du gyur pa gang yin pa de gnyis ni/seng+ge 'di gnyis kho na yin te/de gnyis kyis der skye bo mang po la dud 
4a3(1): 'gro zhes bya ba'i tshig brjod pa'i las de'i rnam par smin pas ni dud 'gror skyes so//de gnyis kyis der tshe gcig tu skyabs su 'gro ba dang /bslab pa'i gzhi rnams bsrungs mas 'chi kar smon lam btab 
4a4(1): pa des ni/dge slong dag nga yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgas 'od srung dang shin tu mnyam pa dang /shugs mnyam pa dang /mdzad pa mnyam pa dang /thabs mnyam pa brnyes nas/'di gnyis kyis nga mnyes par byas 
4a5(1): te/mi mnyes par ma byas pa 'di ni/'das pa'i dus kyi las yin no//da ltar byung ba'i las gang zhe na/de gnyis seng ger gyur nas/nga la sems dpa' bar byas pa des na/lha'i nang du skyes te/'di ni da ltar 
4a6(1): byung ba'i las yin no//snyis bzung zhes bya ba ni gleng gzhi mnyan du yod pa na bzhugs te/de'i tshe mnyan du yod pa na nags khrod zhig na ri dgas kyi rngon pa dag gcig gis ri dgas bshor ba'i phyir/rgya dang snyi dang mjal rlubs dang /
4a7(1): rnyong mang po dag bcugs nas/der ri dgas mang po zhig bzung ngo //rngon pa de dag kyang nang bar sngar langs te/nags khrod der dong nas de dag gis der ri dgas ci zin pa rnams 'das 'phangs te bsad nas/de dag las 
4a8(1): nyung shas cig lus so//de'i tshes phyogs der bcom ldan 'das kyang gshegs so//de nas ri dgas de rnams dang rngon pa de rnams kyis thag ring po zhig nas bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang bcom ldan 'das la 
4a9(1): shin tu dga' bar gyur to//dga' bar gyur nas rngon pa de dag bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der dong ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la phyag 'tshal te/chos nyan pa'i phyir spyan sngar 'dug go/de nas bcom ldan 'das 
4b1(1): kyis de rnams kyi bsam pa dang /bag la nyal dang /khams dang shes pa dang /rang bzhin thugs su chud nas/de dang 'thun pa'i chos bstan te/des na rngon pa de rnams kyis stan de dag nyid la 'khod bzhin du/rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras
4b2(1): bu mngon sum du byas te/de dag gis bden pa mthong nas gsol pa/btsun pa bdag cag legs par gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu 'byung zhing dge slong gi dngos po thob tu rung na/bdag cag kyang bcom ldan 'das
4b3(1): kyi thad du tshangs par spyod pa spyad par 'tshal lo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag dge slong tshur shog ces bya ba'i tshig gis rab tu byung ste/bsnyen par rdzogs par mdzad nas de dag la lung yang phog ste/de dag
4b4(1): gyis kyang brtson pa dang bsgrub pa dang /'bad pas nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs te/dgra bcom pa nyid mngon sum du byas so//ri dgas de rnams kyang bcom ldan 'das la sems dga' bzhin du shi 'phos te dus
4b5(1): las 'das nas lha'i nang du skyes so//lha'i bu 'am/lha'i bu mo skyes ma thag pa ni chos nyid kyis sems rnam pa gsum skye bar 'gyur te/bdag gang nas shi 'phos/gang du skyes/las gang gis skye snyam mo//de
4b6(1): nas dud 'gro la shi 'phos pa dang /lha'i nang du skyes pa dang /bcom ldan 'das la sems gda' bar gyur par de dag gis mthong ngo //de nas de dag gis bsams pa/bdag cag zhag lon nas bcom ldan 'das blta
4b7(1): ba'i phyir 'gro ba ni bdag gi cha ma yin gyis/bdag cag zhag ma lon par bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i phyir 'gro'o snyam mo//de nas sngon ri dgas su gyur pa'i lha'i bu gdub khor dag cing 'gul ba thogs pa/
4b8(1): lus do shal dang /se mo dos brgyan pa/nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyis spras pa'i cod pan can/lus gur ku ma dang /ta ma la'i lob ma dang /sprig ka la sogs pas byugs pa de dag gis/de nyid kyi mtshan mo lha'i
4b9(1): me tog u pa la dang /pad+ma dang /ku mu ta dang /pad+ma dkar po dang /man da ra ba rnams kyis thub bkang ste/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal thams cad 'od chen pos snang bar byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la me tog gis gtor
5a1(1): @#//te;bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas/chos mnyan pa'i phyir bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'khod do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag la de dang 'thun pa'i 
5a2(1): chos bstan te/de dag gis thos nas bden pa mthong bar gyur te/de dag gi bden pa mthong nas/rang gi gnas su dong ngo //de nas dge slong rnams kyis rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal thams cad snang ba chen pos khyab par mthong 
5a3(1): nas;bcom ldan 'das la zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das ci lags/mdang gsum mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa 'am/lha'i dbang po brgya byin nam/'jig rten skyong ba bzhi 'am/lha'i bu rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba gzhan 
5a4(1): zhig bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i slad du mchis lags sam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong bdag mdang sum mi mjed kyi bdag bo tshangs pa 'am/lha'i dbangapo brgya byin nam/'jig rtan skyong ba bzhi 
5a5(1): 'am;lha'i bu rdzu 'phrul che zhingamathu che ba gzhan 'ga' zhig nga blta ba'i phyir 'ongs pa ma yin gyi/dge slong khyed kyis nags khung dgon pa na/ri dgas lnga brgya rngon pa rnams kyis mdas 'phangs te bsad pa mthong ngam/
5a6(1): gsol pa/btsun pa mthong lags so//bka' stsal ba/de dag nga la sems dga' bar gyur bzhin du shi 'phos nas dus (las)'das pa las lha'i nang du skyes te/lhar gyur nas nga'i thad du 'ongs te/de dag la ngas chos 
5a7(1): stan nas/nga las chos thos pa des na/de dag gis bden pa rnams mthong ste/de dag gis bden pa mthong nas rang gi gnas su dong ngo //de nas dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la zhus pa/btsun pa rdon pa/
5a8(1): de dag gi las ci bgyis na/las de'i rnam par smin pas/gdol pa'i rigs su skyes lags/ri dgas de dag gis las ci bgyis na/las de 'i rnam par smin pas ri dgas su skyes nas 'di rnams kyis bcom ldan
5a9(1): 'das ma nyes par bgyis te/mi mnyes par ma gyis lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi gsung rab la 'di dag thams cad rab tu byung ste/dge slong 'thab 
5b1(1): grol can la mi mkhas pa sha stag tu gyur te/dge slong der de rnams kyis gdol pa zhes bya ba'i tshig brjod do//gcig gis dud 'gro zhes bya ba'i tshig brjod do //gang dag gis dud 'gro zhes bya ba tshig brjod pa de 
5b2(1): dag ni dud 'gror skyes so//gang dag gis gdol pa zhes bya ba'i tshi brjod pa de dag ni gdol par skyes so//der tshe gcig tu tshangs par spyod pa spyad nas/dbang po rnams yongs su smin par byas pa des ni/
5b3(1): da ltar rnam par grol bar gyur to//gong ma sreg shes bya ba ni/bcom ldan 'das dge slong nyi rgya lnga bcu'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /tshong pa lnga brgya dang /sprang po lnga brgya dang /thabs gcig tu ljongs rgyu zhing 
5b4(1): gshegs pa las/nags khrod gcig tu lam byung ste/bcom ldan 'das gshegs pa'i tshe/nags khrod de'i nang gi gnas su me chen po 'bar nas/der skye bo phal po che dag 'jigs shing skrag par gyur te/bdag
5b5(1): cag ni tshig zin no//bdag cag ni 'tsho bar 'gyur ba'i skabs med do snyam mo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis skye bo phal tshe dag 'jigs shing skrag pa gzigs so//gzigs nas kyang 'dron po de rnams kyi mdun logs
5b6(1): gshegs nas/tshigs su bcad de bka' stsal pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi stobs gang dang //dgra bcom rnams kyi stobs gang dang //sangs rgyas dkon mchog gzi brjid kyis//me khyod lam ni dbye bar gyis//
5b7(1): sangs rgyas kun gyi stobs ga dang //dgra bcom rnamasakyi stobs gang dang //dam chos dkon mchog gzi brji kyis//me khyod lam nidabye bar gyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi stogs gang dang //dgra bcon rnams kyi stobs gang dang//dge
5b8(1): 'dun dkon mchog gzi brjidakyis//me khyod lam ni bye bar gyis//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces gsungs ma thag tu me zhi bar gyur nas/tshong ba de dag bcom ldan 'das la shin tu dga' bar gyur to//dga' bar gyur nas chos
5b9(1): mnyan pa'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'khod do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag la de dang 'thun pa'i chos bstan te/des na de dag gi nang nas kha cig gis ni dro bar gyur pa bskyed do//kha cig gis ni spyi bo dang bden pa dang
6a1(1): @#//'thun pa'i bzod pa dang /'jig rten gyi chos kyi mchog dang mthong ba'i snyoms par 'jug pa bskyed do//kha cig gis ni rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu mngon sum du byas so//kha cig gis ni lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu mngon
6a2(1): sum du byas so//kha cig gyis ni phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu mngon sum du bas so//kha cig gi ni rab byung nas dgra bcom pa nyid mngon sum du byas so/kha cig gis ni 'khor los sgyur ba'i rgyal por 'gyur ba'i sa bon bskyed do//kha cig gis na stobs kyi
6a3(1): 'khor los sgyur bar 'gyur ba dang /kha cig gis ni brgya byin du 'gyur ba dang /kha cig gyis ni tshangs par 'gyur pa dang /kha cig gis ni nyan thos kyi byang chub dang /kha cig gis ni rang sangs rgyas kyi byang chub dang /kha cag gis ni bla na med pa yang
6a4(1): dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi sa bon bskyed do//'khor de dag gi nang nas phal cher ni sangs rgyas la gzhol/chos la 'bab/dge 'dun la bab par byas te bkod do//de nas dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/btsun pa bcom ldan
6a5(1): 'das kyis skyes bo phal po che zhugs kyis tshig par 'gyur ba las thar bar mdzad nas/g.yung drung gi mthar thug par grub pa dang /bde ba'i mya ngan las 'das pa la bkod pa la gzigs/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/da ltar 'ba' shig ma yin te/
6a6(1): 'dus pa'i dus na yang ngas dud 'gro phal po che/me'i 'jigs pa las bskyabs nas 'jigs pa med pa'i gnas su bkod pa de nyon cig/dge slong dag sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na/nags khrod cig na bya phal mo che gnas so//de nas nags khrod der shing skam po
6a7(1): gcig la gcig drud pa las me byung nas/bya de dag gi nang nas 'gro nus pa de dag ni dong ngo //'gro mi nus pa dang sgo nga rnams ni de nyid du bor ro//de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' gong ma sreg du skyes pa zhig yod de/byang chub sems dpa' des thag ring po zhig
6a8(1): nas me 'bar zhing 'ong bar mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang de sems can de dag la snying rje skyes te/bdag gis sems can sdug bsngal dang phrad pa 'di rnams kyi phyir yang brtson 'grus ma bskyed na/bdag gis sems can rnams 'khor ba las ji ltar bsgral
6a9(1): bar bya snyam du bsams nas/byang chub sems dpa' de mdab ma chur sbangs nas/me chen po steng gi nam mkha' la 'dug ste/ma dab ma sprugs nas tshigs su bcad de smras pa/'di dag mdab ma yod mod kyi//'di dag da dung 'phur mi nus//'di dag rkang
6a10(1): pa yod mod kyi//'di dag de dang 'gro mi nus//'di rnams pha ma 'phur te song //me khyod sdod la ma 'gul cig/bden dang bden tshig gang yin pa//bdag gis byams sems rab bsgoms pa'i//bden pa'i tshig ni 'di yis na//me khyod sdod la ma 'gul shig/de nas
6b1(1): lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis/byang chub sems dpa' sems can gyi don gyi phyir ni dga' zhing 'dug pa mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang des bsams pa/'di ni bskal pa bzang po'i byang chub sems dpa' yin te/sems can la phan pa'i phyir ni dga' zhing 'dug gis/
6b2(1): ma la bdag gis grogs bya'o snyam nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyi char mo che phab ste/me de zhi bar gyur nas/sems can de dag thams cad me'i 'jigs pa las thar bar gyur te/dge slong dag ji snyam du sems/de'i tshe bya gong ma sreg du gyur pa byang chub
6b3(1): sems dpa'i spyod pa la gnas pa gang yin pa de ni nga nyid yin no//de'i tshe nags khrod de na gnas pa'i sems can du gyur pa de dag ni skye bo phal po che 'di dag kho na yin te/de'i tshe yang ngas de dag me'i 'jigs pa las thar par byas te/'jigs pa med pa'i gnasu
6b4(1): bkod do//da ltar yang ngas 'jigs pa las thar bar byas nas/g.yung drung gi mthar thug pa grub pa dang byed pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa la bkod do//gzhan yang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'od srung gi gsung rab la 'di dag thams cad kha cig ni rab tu
6b5(1): byung ngo//kha cig ni dge bsnyen du gyur te/der 'di rnams kyis tshe gcig tu tshangs par spyod pa'i gnas bsrungs nas/dbang po rnams yongs su smin par byas pa des na/de ltar rnam par grol bar gyur to//pha zhes bya ba ni gleng bzhi rgyal po'i khab na bzhugs te/de'i
6b6(1): tshe rgyal po'i khab na khyim bdag nor can zhes bya ba phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che ba yongasu 'dzin pa yangs shing rgya che ba rnam thos kyi bu'i nor dang mnyam pa/rnam thos kyi bu'i nor dang 'gran pa zhig gnas pa des/thabs zlar bab pa las chung ma
6b7(1): blangs so//de de dang lhan cig rtse zhing dga' la dga' dgur spyod pa las phyi zhig na de'i chung ma la bu chags nas/de zla ba dgu 'am bcu lon pa dang /khye'u gzugs bzang zhing blta na sdug la mdzes pa/mdog bzang po gser gyi mdog can mgo bo
6b8(1): gdugs ltar 'dug pa lag pa ring ba/dpral bas dbyes che ba/sna mtho ba/sna legs pa/smin ma stug pa zhig btsas te/khye'u de btsas ma thag tu skye bo phal po ches blta na sdug par gyur to//de nas de'i btsas ston rgyas par byas nas/khye'u
6b9(1): 'di'i ming ji skad gdags shes ming 'dogs par byed de/khye'u 'di btsas ma thag tu skye bo pha po ches bltas na/sdug par gyur pas na/de'i phyir khye'u 'di'i ming blta na sdug ces gdags so zhes zer ro//de nas khye'u blta na sdug pa 'o ma dang /
7a1(1): //zho dang mar dang /zhun mar dang /mar gyi snying kus bskyed srings nas /rgyal bo'i khyab thams cad de'i grags pa rgyas bar gyurd to //de nas gang gyi tshe bcom ldan 'das kyis
7a2(1): ye shes bstes nas/mthard kyis gtul bar bya ba'i don mdzad cing rgyal po'i khab tu byon ba'i tshe /der bcom ldan 'das kyis 'od ma'i tshal du zhugs nas sems can stong phrag du ma skyabs su 'gre ba dang
7a3(1): pa'i gzhi rnams la bkod do //skye bo mang bo de dag kyang sde mtshan nas ste mtshan dang /pha tshan nas pha tshan dang /dag sde 'ga' ster dus dus su bcom ldan 'das ba lta ba'i phyir 'ong bar byed de /phyin
7a4(1): ba'i phyir 'ong bar myi byed do //de nas phyi zhig na khye'u blta na sdug gyis dris pa //sngon chad ni skye bo mang bo 'di dag da blta ba'i /phyir 'ong na da ni da lta pa'i phyir myi 'ong ba ji las gyurd //de dag
7a5(1): smras pa/de dag ni da ltar bcom ldan 'das blta ba'i phyir 'dor bar byed do //khye'us dris pa /bcom ldan 'das shes bya de lta bu de su zhig /de nas pha ma gnyis kyis de la bcom ldan 'das
7a6(1): pa rgyas bar brjod pa //de nas de thos ma thag tu khye'u de ngo mtshar du gyurd te /bdag pas ches blta na sdug ba de ci lta bu zhig snyam nas /pha mas gnyis la smras pa /yab yum bdag bcom ldan 'das de
7a7(1): ba'i slad du mchi bar 'tshal lo //pha ma gnyis kyis smras pa/bu thabs gcig tu dong ngo zhes smras pa //pha ma gnyis kyis khye'u khrid de 'od ma'i tshal du song nas khye'u des thag ring bo zhig nas /sangs
7a8(1): rgyas bcom ldan 'das sku skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis kyis legs bar brgyand cing sba bar byas pa /mye lus kyi babyibs su 'dug pa/mye lce mar gyis btab ba lta bu /mar mye gser gyi snod
7a9(1): du bzhag pa lta bu /mchod sdod rin po che sna tshogs kyis sras pa lta bu /blo gsal zhing dri ma myed la /rnam bar dag pa la dang ldan ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo //mthong nas kyang bcom ldan 'das
7b1(1): pa mchog tu dga' ba skyes nas de la pha'i 'du shes kyang byung ste des pha pha zhes sgra phyung nas bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyind nas /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag tshab te /pha tsam du 'dzind cing bcom
7b2(1): ldan 'das kyi zhabs drung du 'dug go //de'i pha ma gnyis kyang bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs pa mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas /chos mnyand pa'i spyan sngar 'dug go //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de dag gyi rkyen du
7b3(1): 'tsham ba'i chos bstand nas cang myi gsung bar zhugs so//de nas khyim bdag nor can dang /de'i chung mas bcom ldan 'das kyi gtam mjug rdzogs bar rig nas /khye'u 'i lag pa nas bzung ste 'gro zhes byas na
7b4(1): khye'u de bcom ldan 'das kyi thad nas bral bar ma nus so//de nas pha ma gnyis kyis bsams ba/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag gyi khyim du spyan drangs dang /thabs des na 'di bdag cag gyis khyim du bkri nus
7b5(1): 'gyur ro snyam nas /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyard pa btud de /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to /dgung bdun gyi bar du bcom ldan 'das bdag cagagyi khyim du
7b6(1): su gsol bar ji gnang zhes byas so //de nas bcom ldan 'dis kyis khyim bdag de la cang myi gsung bar gnang ngo //de nas khyim bdag des bcom ldan 'das kyi cang myi gsung bar
7b7(1): bar rig nas /chung ma la smras pa/bzang mo song la bcom ldan 'das kyi zhal zas sta gon gyis shig bdag ni khye'u dang lhan cig tu 'dug go zhes byas nas /khyim bdag de khye'u de dang lhan cig
7b8(1): rgyal byed kyi tshal du mtshan mo dgongs ma la btab bo //chung mas kyang de nyid kyi mtshan mo bza' ba dang bca' ba gtsang ma bzang ngo mang bo spyar nas nang bar sngar langs te bdan shams nas /chu ra bkang ste ba
7b9(1): bcom ldan 'das la phrin gyis gdugs tshod bskul ba dang /btsun pa gdugs tshod 'bab ste /bshos lags na /bcom ldan 'das kyis da de'i dus la 'bab bar dgongs te gshegs su 
8a1(1): //der gshegs te byon nas /bcom ldan 'das dge slong gyi dge 'dun gyi gur la gdan shams pa la zhugs so /de nas khyim bdag des bcom ldan 'das la stsogs pa dge slong gyi dge 'dun rnams bde'
8a2(1): bar zhugs bar rig nas /rang gi lag nas bza' ba dang bca' ba gtsang ma bzang pos tshim bar byas te /ji bzhed pa bstabs nas /bcom ldan 'das bshos gsol zind te gsang ster ni rgyu /phyag ni bcabs bar rig nas
8a3(1): stan ches dma' ba zhig blangs te chos mnyand pa'i phyir khord dang bcas te /bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar khrod do /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i bsam ba dang /bag la nyal dang khams dang shes pa dang /rang bzhin thugs 
8a4(1): su chud r+g+gas /de dang mthun ba'i chos bstand te /des na khyim bdag de khor dang /bcas bas bstand te de dag nyid la khrod bzhin du 'jigs tshogs la lta ba'i ri'i rtse mo mthon po nyi shu ye shes rdo rjes bcom nas rgyund du zhugs
8a5(1): pa'i bras bu mngon gsum du byas so //de ltar bden ma mthong nas /khye'u //des pha ma gnyis la gsold te /bcom ldan 'das ga la ba /de logs su thal mo sbyar pa btud de bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces
8a6(1): gsold to //btsun ba bdag legs bar gsungs pa'i chos 'dul ba la rab tu byung ba dang /bsnyen pa rdzogs shing dge slong gyi dngos po thob tu rung na /bdag kyang bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du tshangs bar spyod pa 
8a7(1): spyad bar 'tshal lo //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong tshur shog ces bya ba'i tshig gis rab tu phyung ste bsnyen pa rdzogs bar mdzad nas /de la lung yang phogs ste des kyang brtson ba dang bsgrub pa dang /'bad bas
8a8(1): nyon mongs ba thams cad spangs nas dgra bcom ba nyid mngon gsum du byas te /de dgra bcom ba khams gsum pa'i 'dod chags dang bral bar gyurd nas /gser dang bor bar mnyam ba /nam mkha' dang /lag thil du 'dra 
8a9(1): ba'i sems dang ldan ba /tsan dan spangs ba lta bur gsil gyurd nas /rig bas sgo nga'i sbubs bcom ba rig pa dang mngon bar shes ba dang /so so yang dag bar rig pa thob ba /srid ba'i rnyed pa dang chags pa dang 
8a10(1): bkur sti la myi lta ba /dbang po dang nye dbang dang bcas ba'i lha rnams kyi mchod cing rjed pa dang gus bar smra ba'i gnas su gyurd to //de nas dge slong rnams kyis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das la gsold pa /btsun ba
8b1(1): bcom ldan 'das byis ba stong phrag du mas mthor yang su yang 'di kho na ltar bcom ldan 'das la 'jar mar myi bgyid lags so //bcom ldan 'das kyis khye 'u 'di ni nga'i 'das ma thag pa'i tshe rabs lnga brgyar bur gyurd pa na
8b2(1): sngon gyi bag chags de kho nas 'di nga la 'jar byed do/gsold pa khye 'u 'dis las de'i rnam par smyind bas phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che ba'i rigs kyi bu skyes te /'di'i gzugs bzang zhing lta na rag pa mdzes 
8b3(1): bar gyurd la /'dis bcom ldan 'das kyang gnyis bar bgyis nas /myi gnyes bar ma bgyis te bcom ldan 'das kyi stand pa la rab tu byung nas /nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs te /dgra bcom ba nyid mngon
8b4(1): gsum du bgyis lags /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsald pa /smon lam gyi dbang las gyurd to //gsold pa btsun ba 'dis smon lam gar tu btab lags //bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' bstsald pa 
8b5(1): dge slong dag sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na /bskald ba bzang bo 'di nyid la skye dgu'i tshe lo bzhi khri thub pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig pa dang zhabs su ldan ba /bde' bar gshegs pa 
8b6(1): 'jig rten khyend ba /skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba /bla na myed pa/lha dang myi rnams kyi stond pa /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'khor ba 'jig 'jig rten du byung ste /der gang gyi tshe yang 
8b7(1): dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 'khor ba 'jig gyis bla na myed pa'i ye shes bsnyes nas rgyal bo'i pho brang mdzes ldan du gshegs pa de'i tshe /rgyal bo mdzes bas yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgas 'khor ba 'jig gyi phyir 
8b8(1): gtsug lag khang rnam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa zhig byas nas /yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sadas rgyas 'khor ba zhig la phul lo /rgyal bo mdzes pa de'i lugs kyis na nyin cig bzhin du 'khord dang bcas te
8b9(1): gtsug lag khang du 'gro zhing gtsug lag khang gyi byi dor byed do //de nas phyir zhig na rgyal bo mdzes pa de gtsug lag khang du song ba las /bya ba zhig byung nas /des pu thu po bskos te //der khyod kyis 
8b10(1): gtsug lag khang gyi byi dor gyis shig ces bsgo nas rgyal po song ngo //de nas rgyal bo'i 'khord dang bcas pa gtsug lag khang gyi byi dod byed pa la zhugs pa las /yang dag bar rdzog ba'i sadas rgyas 'khor ba 'jig
9a1(1): /gyis ji ltar rgyal bo'i bu des nyi ma nub kyi bar du yar gtsug lag khang gyi byi dor byed myi nus pa de lta bur byin gyis brlabs nas/de gtsug lag khang de nyid du dgongs mal btab bo//de der dgongs mal
9a2(1): btab nas dge slong rnams cho 'phrul dag byed pa yang mthong brgya byin dang /tshangs pa la stsogs pa 'ong ba yang mthong ngo /de nas de mthong ma thag tu de bcom ldan 'das la de shind tu dga' bar gyurd nas/gtsug lag de nyid
9a3(1): na dge slong dag gyi rim gro byed pa la brtson par gyurd te/phyir khyim du 'gro myi 'dod par gyurd to//de der lo lnga stong gyi bar du dge slong rnams la rim 'gro byed cing 'dug ste/byams pa'i 'ating nge 'dzind yang skyed nas des
9a4(1): tshe cig tu skyabs su 'gro ba dang bslab pa'i gzhi rnams srungs nas/'chi kar smon lam btab pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis na bdag gang dang gang tu skye bar gyurd pa de dang /der gzugs bzang zhing lta na sdug la mdzes
9a5(1): par gyurd cig/bdag gyis stond pa 'di lta bu mnyes par byed par gyurd cig/myi mnyes par byed par ma gyurd cig de kho na'i stan pa la rab tu byung nas nyon mongs pa thams cad spangs te/dgra bcom pa
9a6(1): nyid mngon gsum du byed par gyurd ci ces byas so//dge slong dag/ji snyam du sems/de'i tshe rgyal bo'i bur gyurd pa gang yin pa de ni khye'u 'di kho na yin te/des der yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs
9a7(1): rgyas 'khor 'jig gyi nyan thos kyi dge 'dun rnams la kur byas nas/smon lam btab pa'i las de'i rnam par smyind pas gang dang gang tu skyes pa de dang der phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che ba'i rigs su skyes te/da
9a8(1): dge slong dag nga yang dag par rdzogs pa'i  sangs rgyas 'khor ba 'jig dang /shind tu mnyam pa dang /shugs mnyam pa dang /thabs mnyam pa dang /thabs pa mnyam pa bsnyes par gyurd nas 'dis nga mnyes byas te/
9a9(1): myi mnyes pas ma byas la/nga nyid kyi stand pa la rab tu byung nas nyin mongs pa thams cad sbangs te/dgra bcom pa nyid mngon gsum du byas so//des der byams pa sgoms pa'i las de'i rnam par sbyind pa des ni/gang dang
9a10(1): gang tu skyes pa de dang deng gzugs bzang zhing lta na sdug la mdzes par gyurd to//gzhan yang yang dag par rdzogs pa'is sangs rgyas 'od srung gyi gsung rab la 'di rab tu byung ste/der 'dis tshe gcig tu tshangs par spyod pa spyad de/
9b1(1): dbang po rnams yongs su smyind par byas pa des na /da ltar rnam par 'grol bar gyurd to////myi rgod ces bya ba ni /gleng gzhi mnyand tu yod pa na zhugs te /de'i tshe mnyand tu yod pa dang /
9b2(1): rgyal bo'i khab kyi bar tshams na shing sa la'i nags khung na myi rgod lnga brgya tsam zhig 'khra zhing gnas te /de dag de na 'khod pas skye bo mang bo kha 'dogs par byed do //de nas phyi zhig na 'dron bo mang bo dag cig bcom nas tshong pa 
9b3(1): rnams rgyal bo gsal rgyal gyi thad du dong ste rgyal bo la gsol nas /rgyal bo gsal rgyal gyi bu 'phags skyes bo dbung gyi tshogs yan lag bzhi dang bsgos te der btang nas /des myi rgod lnga brgya bo de dag gson bor bzung ste 'ongs 
9b4(1): nas rgyal bo gsal rgyal la 'phul lo /de nas rgyal bo gsal rgyal gyis myi rgod lnga brgya bsod du btang ste /gshed ma rnams kyis myi rgod lnga brgya bo de ngag gsad pa'i sar khrid do //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bgrod cig pa'i
9b5(1): lam stond pa/rnam pa gnyis shes bya dang ye shes la mnga' brnyes pa/ma dres pa'i dran ba nye bar gzhag pa gsum la mkhas pa /myi 'jigs pa bzhis myi 'jigs pa /'gro ba lngar 'gro ba las rnam par 'grol ba /
9b6(1): skye mched drug la mkhas pa /byang chub kyi yan lag bdun la spyod yul ba /rnam par thard pa brgyad la bsam gtan ba /mthard kyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la snyoms par 'jug pa /
9b7(1): stobs bcu'i stobs dang ldan ba yang dag pa'i seng ge'i sgra chen bo sgrogs pa rnams ni /chos nyid kyis nyin dus sum tshan dus gsum ste /nyin mtshan drug tu sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyis 'jig rten la gzigs te /
9b8(1): su ni rgud /su ni dar /su ni 'phongs shing 'dug/su ni nyam nga bar gyurd /su ni gnod pa dang ldan /su ni 'phongs pa dang nyam nga ba dang ldan/su ni ngan song du zhol /su ni ngan song du 'bab /sa ni ngan song
9b9(1): ra 'bab /su ni ngas ngan song nas phyung ste mtho' ris dang thard pa dang 'bras bu la gzhag par bya /su ni nye bar spyod pa'i 'dam du 'bying ba las lag nas drang bar bya /su ni 'phags pa'i nor bdun myed pa las /
9b10(1): 'phags pa'i nor bdun gyi dbang phyug la dbang byed du zhug par bya /su ni dge' ba'i rtsa ba rnams ma skyed pa las bskyed par bya /su ni dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams skyed zind pa las /yongs su smyind par bya 
10a1(1): //su ni dge' ba'i rtsa ba rnams yongs su smyind pa las ye shes kyi mtshon gyis brton bar bya /su la ni sangs rgyas byung bas brgyand pa'i 'jig rten 'bras bu yod bar bya snyam du ye shes gzigs
10a2(1): pa 'jug go //rgya mtsho chu srin rnams kyi gnas //dus rlabs yol bar 'gyur yang srid //'dul bar bya ba'i sras rnams la //sangs rgyas 'dus las g.yol ba myed //de nas bcom ldan
10a3(1): 'das kyis myi rgod lnga brgya gtul ba'i 'dus la 'bab bar dgongs nas//rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal nas myi snang bar gyurd te rgyal bo'i khab tu gshegs so //de nas rgyal bo gsal rgyal gyis thag ring bo zhig nas
10a4(1): bcom ldan 'das mthong ngo /mthong nas kyang bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste /phyind nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te /bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsold to /
10a5(1): bcom ldan 'das ci la gshegs khags /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' bstsald pa /rgyal po chen po rigs kyi bu lnga brgya bo de dag ma bsad bar mthong shig rgyal bos gsold pa /gal te
10a6(1): rab tu byung na gtang bar 'tshal lo zhes gsol nas //de nas rgyal bo gsal rgyal kyis myi rgod lnga brgya bo de dag phyir zlog ste bcom ldan 'das la phul lo //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis
10a7(1): de rnams rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal du khrid nas /rab tu phyung ste bsnyend pa rdzogs bar mdzad nas de dag la lung yang phog ste /de dag gyis kyang rtson pa dang /bsgrubs pa dang /'bad bas nyon mongs
10a8(1): pa thams cad spangs te /dgra bcom pa nyid mngon gsuM du byas so //de nas dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsold pa //bcom ldan 'das kyis myi rgod lnga brgya srog phangs pa stsald te /
10a9(1): ga bu rung gyi thar thug gyi grub pa dang bde' ba'i mya ngan las 'das pa la bkod pa la gzigs //bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' bstsald pa /da ltar 'ba' shig ma yin te /'das pa'i dus na yang
10a10(1): ngas rigs kyi bu lnga brgya bo 'di rnams srog phangs pa byin te /ji ltar bsam gtan bzhi dang /mngon bar shes pa lnga la bkod pa de nyon cig //dge slong dag sngon byung ba 'das pa'i 'dus na /yul
10b1(1): byi de ha zhes bya ba na rgyal bo dbang can sde zhes bya ba de rgyal bo byed do //de nas phyi zhig na rgyal bo des dpung gyi tshogs yan lag bzhi 'go skon nas /ri dgas shor du song ba las rgyal bo de la blon bo lnga brgyas glags
10b2(1): btsal te /de dag bsams ba da ni rgyal bo 'di bdag cag gyi lag na 'dug gyis /'di ni 'dug pa kho na'i tshe /bdag cag gyis 'di bsad do snyam mo //'jig rten gyi dgra dang zun gyi nang na 'dug ste /der gzhan
10b3(1): dag gyis rgyal bo la sbran nas de thos ma thag tu rgyal bo dbang can sdes blon bo lnga brgya bo de dag bsod du btang ste de dag gshed ma rnams kyis 'khrid nas nags khrod cig tu zhugs te /gsad ces byas pa las nags khrod de na
10b4(1): drang srong zhig nas te /drang srong des blon bo lnga brgya po de dag rgyal bo dbang cen sde la gtong du bcug nas dka' thub kyi gnas su khrid de rab tu phyung ngo /rab tu phyung nas de dag gyis kyang bsam gtan bzhi dang /mngon bar shes

73a2(1): 'phrin rnaMs dus bzhin du bskyal te/de las de dang yang dag bar blangs pa de lta bu dang /yang dag bar bzung ba de lta bus lus zhig nas dga' ldan gyi lha rnams dang bskal ba mnyam bar skye'o zhes bya rgas rgyas bar sbyar te/de de nas shi 'phos

73b1(1): byed cing lto ba myi che ba dang /lto bdun myi byed pa dang /lto bdun gyi sbyor ba myi rtson ba dang /gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' 'di lta ste/zas dang skom dang /bca ba dang /bza' ba dang /gos dang spos dang /me tog dang /phreng ba dang

93a2(1): de dag la /mun pa mun nag snang ba myed pa /mun nag mar me myed bar /mar mye'i 'bru mar dang /mar me'i snying bo dang /mar me'i snod rnams byin ba dang /sgron ma rnams dang /mar mye rnams dang /snang ba rnams

158b4(1): kyi shing yongs su sa brtol zhes bya ba zhig yod de /yongs su sa rtol gyi rtsa ba ni dpag tshad lnga thur du zug go//'phang du ni dpag tshad brgya yod de /yal ga phyir gyes pa ni dpag tshad lnga bcu yod do /yongs su sa rtol gyi

190b6(1): mnyam ba nyid do/dus gsum gyi rang bzhin yin pas ci lta ba bzhi na du mnyam ba nyid do/ma rig pa dang srid pa dang srid pa'i rang bzhin yin bsam rig pa dang rnam par 'grol bar mnyam ba/nyid do/'khor
190b7(1): ba'i rang bzhin yin bas mya ngan las 'das par mnyam ba nyid do/de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid du mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas bas//de'i phyir yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas shes bya'o

190b9(1): rab tu stond te//de'i chos de yang de byed pa'i sdug sngal yang dag par zad par bya ba'i phyir nges bar 'byung ngo//gang dag stond pa ma yin bar stond pa'i zhes dam 'cha' ba dang/yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sadas rgyas ma yin bar
190b10(1): yang dag bar rdzogs pa'i sanas rgyas so zhes dam 'cha' ba de dag de bzhin gshegs pa'i myi 'jigs pa 'dis zild kyis nond par gyur zhing myed bar yang 'gro bar 'gyur te/'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i las bcu gcig

191a2(1): dang slob dang myi slob chos rnams dang //rang rgyal chos rnams gang yin dang //de dag sangs rgyas chos dang mnyam//'jig rten pa dang de bzhin du //'jig rten las ni 'das pa dang //dge dang myi dge ba gang yin pa//mya ngan
